(Also: surface climbs took twenty minutes more of the run, waiting in shelters eight.)
360. *mid-231-j, three hours less ten and no Nether; ended on a flip between the cast and the portal step.* The night mine took 37 minutes of the run, the food search 18, surface climbs 20. One stall is fixed: a staircase up a mountain toward (-730, 162, 69) found "no tool for snow block" on four of its steps and rested ten minutes. A block soft enough to dig by hand in a moment (hardness under one: snow, dirt, sand and the like) is now dug on a staircase without its tool, and the staircase's digs no longer ask for the drop; the room is what a stair wants.
361. *mid-205-k, "slain by Zombie" at minute 80, eleven seconds after a restart.* The bots were restarted for note 360 while this one held a pillar at y -8 among zombies; it rejoined in the same crowd with nothing decided, fell to y -10 and was killed in eleven seconds. It is the fifth death of the day to follow a restart by seconds (a boat twice, notes 338 and 357; a span, 353; a hoglin, 358; this). A new build is now taken at a quiet moment: the restart is asked for by touching .bot-state/restart-requested, and each bot quits for it once nothing hostile is within sixteen blocks and it stands on dry ground, off any span or seat, or after five minutes anyway (quiet-restart.js); the supervisor starts the new build as before.
362. *mid-235-j, "fireballed by Ghast" at minute 85, sixteen minutes into its Nether (reached at 69).* A minute after a restart, the fortress search stood at the end of its span at y 41 over the lava sea with no blocks left to build with (sixteen gravel, two planks); a ghast thirty-seven blocks off shot, the walls (note 332) could not go up for want of blocks, and it held still five times until the fireball threw it off, eleven blocks into the lava. With a shot or a biter pressing on a span and no walls to be had, the bot now steps back off the span to firm ground within eight blocks (an emergency, as the step out of lava is).
363. *mid-241-h, "blown up by Creeper" at minute 43, on a ledge at y 74.* At 8.9 health, hunting at night, the bot came onto a one-wide ledge; a creeper walked up, the span's rule held it still and crouched, and the creeper went off beside it. Walls do not stop a blast, and holding still is where a creeper lights. With a creeper coming within twelve blocks on a span, the bot now steps off it first, to firm ground at least two blocks farther from the creeper than it stands (note 362's step off, pointed away).
364. *mid-205-l, "suffocated in a wall" at minute 6.* Twenty seconds after rejoining (a quiet restart), the bot stood at (-368.9, 67, 577.5) in an open two-high cell and took a point of suffocation every half second for eight seconds, twenty to none, nothing dug. The game tests suffocation with a box round the eye four fifths of the body's width, and at x -368.9 that box reached into gravel in the next column (x -370); the bot's test looked at the eye's own cell only, which was open. The test is now the game's: every cell the eye's box touches, and the solid one found is the one dug out.
365. *mid-207-g, "shot by Skeleton" at minute 84 (not explained).* On a pillar of its own two blocks up at (25, 72, 296), a skeleton seven to twelve blocks off shooting every three seconds: the charge was chosen (set aside, no firm ground toward it from the pillar top), then the retreat twice, told a way was found ten blocks off, 1.8 seconds at a run. The retreat's walks stalled three times, three seconds each, forward held, sneak not, and the body hardly moved (velocity 0.02 to 0.07 a tick) from a pillar top with open air on every side; eleven health to none. What held the walk is not in the record: the crouch was off; a raised shield slows a walk to a fifth, and the shield state is not recorded. The flight record's keys now include "shield" while the shield is raised.
366. *mid-227-j, "blown up by Creeper" at minute 67 (not explained).* Thirty-eight seconds after a quiet restart, pillaring up toward its portal at y 103 from (-151, 64, -623), the work stopped for a creeper four blocks off (20:59), and in the three seconds before it went off nothing was reported by the survival layer: no stance asked, no back-off, one navigation that stalled at the pillar's foot (toward (-153, 66, -624), whose it is not in the record). Twenty to none at point blank. Why the survival layer made no answer to a creeper at four blocks is not in the record; watched for.
367. *mid-211-l, "shot by Pillager" at minute 44, by a stream (not fixed).* A pillager patrol, four crossbows six to eight blocks off, arrows about once a second: the rule's charge ran once, Jev chose a pillar and then a retreat (neither went: the bot was in and out of the water, swimming across), and by 5.5 health only the pocket and keeping on were left on offer, every other stance having failed here in the last twenty seconds. Sealed, it was shot through the building. Four shooters at once and water underfoot left no stance that worked; what would have served (out of their sight behind the bank) was the retreat, which found no route in the water.
368. *mid-202-i, "slain by Zombie" at minute 74, at a shelter being built at y 102.* Two zombies; the shelter chosen for the night was walked to with one hitting from behind, and at the site the emergency rule sealed the half-built walls, four seconds of building with a zombie one to two blocks off, from 12.7 to 7.2, because its "no sealing with a mob at arm's length" counted only mobs in sight and the zombie was round the wall. Then the fight, six critical swings in six seconds, too late. A biter within arm's length now rules out sealing whether it is in view or not. (The swings are in the record now as the defend frames' swing kind; the shield's being up is in the keys, note 365.)
369. *mid-242-l, "shot by Piglin" at minute 125, twenty-five minutes into its Nether (fortress reached at 105).* In iron with no gold worn, crossbow piglins went for it at y 42; the retreat found no way, and the rule's "hold defensive position" stood under their arrows forty seconds, from 6.7 to none. At the crossing Jev had chosen to cross without gold every time it was asked, and the gold option it was shown began "undefined Gold: no piece of golden armour carried": its first sentence, the one that says what the option does, was missing from the kit's texts. It now says: make golden boots first and wear them, four gold ingots, smelted from raw gold or gold ore. (Why no stance but holding was left at the end: the retreat and the fight had failed within twenty seconds; not changed.)
370. *mid-244-l, "blown up by Creeper" at minute 42, at y 36.* Stalking a skeleton at 6.7 health with a creeper, a zombie and two skeletons about, the step off an edge (a deadly drop near) chose the cell beside the feet, (49, 36, 120) from 48.8: the walk to it came back at once each time without moving, and the step was taken a hundred times a second until an arrow and the blast. Note 339 ruled out the cell stood on; this was the next cell over, and the walk went nowhere all the same. A step off the edge that does not move the bot now sets itself aside for five seconds, and the rest of the turn (the stance, the swing) answers.
371. *mid-229-h, "burned to death" at minute 143, twenty-one minutes into its Nether (reached at 122).* On a fortress leg the path ran along a one-wide netherrack ridge at y 35, the lava lake at 30 and 31 below on the one side; the bot walked it upright and slid off at z -81.5 with nothing pushing it, into the lava, out of it at 6.8 and burned from there to none. The edge's cost (note 217) still takes a ridge that is the way. While a walk is under way in the Nether, the bot now crouches on any cell beside a drop into lava or one that would take half its health or more, as a player does on a ridge (a crouching body cannot step off an edge), and lets go of the crouch before a step down the path means to take.
372. *mid-202-j, "blown up by Creeper" at minute 17, at full health.* Walling a pocket for the night, the build stopped for a creeper that would go off before it closed (note 292's check), and returned; the next tick began the same pocket and stopped again, a hundred times a second, nothing else answering the creeper, until it went off: twenty to none. The pocket given up for a creeper racing it is now set aside for ten seconds, and the shelter step does not begin a pocket so set aside, so the turn goes to the creeper.
373. *mid-207-h, a loop at minute 28: every way to lava resting.* The lava fetch for the cast had its pool and all four headings to the deep lava resting, and threw "every way to lava from here is resting" three passes running. Note 346 gave the ore descent eight headings at twenty-four and forty-eight blocks; the lava fetch and the obsidian step kept four. They now take the same sixteen, near first (tunneling.js descentTargets).
374. *mid-244-j, three hours and no Nether: thirty-eight minutes carrying lava a bucket at a time.* The cast frame at (48, 71, 129) was at the surface, the walk to the lava beside it given up (three walks came no nearer), and the lava sixty blocks down: with one bucket, ten trips. Making buckets was offered twice, as "twelve more from the 38 iron ingots, about 1 trip against 10", and Jev chose the cast as it was both times; by the later asks the iron had gone elsewhere. The offer now makes no more buckets than the lava still to fetch wants (nine here, not twelve), and says each trip's time to the nearest known lava and the minutes of walking either way. (Also: finding lava pools 15 minutes, food 14.)
375. *mid-244-m, "impaled by Drowned" at minute 38, at a river.* Climbing out to the surface at 8.9 health with a drowned throwing tridents: the shield came up to each one and went straight down, a hundred times a second, and two tridents took it from 8.9 to none. The guard rose for a shot within twenty blocks and held only while one was within twelve, so a trident twelve to twenty off raised and dropped it at once, blocking nothing. It now holds while the shot is still coming at the reach it rose for (to the hold's end).
376. *mid-244-o, "speared by Zombie" two minutes in, at y 14.* Spear zombies at 6.6 health: the pillar went up two, as told "zombies cannot reach a player two up", and a spear's thrust reached the bot on top three times, 6.6 to none. A spear reaches past an arm. The pillar now counts mobs with a spear among those that still reach it two up, in its cost, and says so: "A spear reaches past an arm: the zombie with a spear still reaches the bot two up."
